# Third-party notices

OmniDiff is built from the Rust crates listed below, each under its own license, as declared by
its authors. This file is generated by `make third-party-notices` (cargo-about) from Cargo.lock
and ships beside the binary in every distribution; its notices must travel with any copy of the
software. The full text of every license follows the overview.

OmniDiff itself is licensed under the GNU Affero General Public License, version 3 or later (see
LICENSE), or under a commercial license (see LICENSE-COMMERCIAL).

## Overview

- Apache License 2.0: 224 crates
- MIT License: 131 crates
- BSD 3-Clause &quot;New&quot; or &quot;Revised&quot; License: 8 crates
- Unicode License v3: 5 crates
- BSD Zero Clause License: 1 crate
- BSD 2-Clause &quot;Simplified&quot; License: 1 crate
- zlib License: 1 crate
- bzip2 and libbzip2 License v1.0.6: 1 crate
